THIS WRIT PETITION IS FILED UNDER ARTICLES
226 & 227 OF THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA PRAYING TO
DECLARE THAT THE 97TH AMENDMENT TO THE
CONSTITUTION BY INSERTING PART IXB TO THE
CONSTITUTION OF INDIA IS IN VIOLATION OF ARTICLE
368(2) OF THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA AND ALSO
AGAINST THE BASIC STRUCTURE OF THE CONSTITUTION
OF INDIA ARTICLE 246(3) OF THE CONSTITUTION OF
INDIA.
THIS WRIT PETITION COMING ON FOR ORDERS
THIS DAY, THE COURT MADE THE FOLLOWING:
ORDER
Smt. Rashmi Patel, learned HCGP submits that in
this petition, petitioners have prayed for only voting rights.
Elections have been conducted on 23.02.2020 and nothing
further survives for consideration. Therefore, this petition
is rendered infructuous and accordingly prayed for its
dismissal.
2. Her submission is placed on record. In the
circumstance, this petition is dismissed as having become

246
infructuous reserving liberty to petitioners to seek revival
of the petition, if so advised.
 No costs.

 Sd/-
 JUDGE
